# 概率模型、过程与尾界

可按题名、原件哈希或技能名检索。

## A Class of Delay Optimal Control Problems and Viscosity Solutions to Associated Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman Equations

- 原件 1708bf185e19a977958e2cb99b6e74432c88b4fe27e5c1e4d531018c51d69c41。arXiv:1507.04112v1 (2015-07-15; PDF dated 2018-09-19)。物理页 1, 2, 5, 14。
- deterministic delay HJB。Abstract and HJB formulation on physical pp. 1–2; state well-posedness Theorem 2.2 on p. 5; uniqueness discussion p. 14.。
- 邻近材料。For a controlled deterministic delay equation, it introduces a viscosity notion for the associated infinite-dimensional HJB and identifies the value function as unique under its hypotheses.。
- 适用范围。There is no stochastic forcing. The Lipschitz hypothesis on the delay dynamics and the history-space/norm construction used in Theorem 2.2 are required for the stated result.。

## A Convergence Theorem for Crandall–Lions Viscosity Solutions to Path-Dependent Hamilton–Jacobi–Bellman PDEs

- 原件 f782a857e89b20a7790e2aec27e1e1728e5998adda023a374ba7eab236ed0a3f。arXiv:2304.08919v1。物理页 1, 2。
- path-dependent HJB viscosity-solution convergence。p. 1 abstract; p. 2, Theorem 2.5 description。
- 邻近材料。For compact convergence of the specified bounded/Lipschitz path-dependent input data and compact action space, the source states compact convergence of the corresponding Crandall–Lions viscosity solutions.。
- 适用范围。This is a path-space stability result, not an all-purpose comparison theorem and not a direct result for jump/nonlocal operators.。

## A Family of Martingales Generated by a Process with Independent Increments

- 原件 0a3b11fc58defeac66f91e8a625ddedd014ed2e64763d2bb5d24221325cbddf0。arXiv:0711.2879v1 (19 Nov 2007)。物理页 1, 2。
- Teugels martingales for additive processes。PDF p. 1, abstract and introduction; p. 2, Lévy–Itô representation。
- 邻近材料。It constructs polynomial martingales for additive processes via moment-cumulant polynomials and Teugels martingales associated with jumps.。
- 适用范围。The construction assumes independent increments, continuity in probability, and all moments; it does not supply a compensator theorem for general state-dependent jump processes.。

## A Gel'fand-type spectral radius formula and stability of linear constrained switching systems

- 原件 3c9937944470e5daff84dab497a93837aeb1efff7099cc1adc44f2716dbccd46。arXiv:1107.0124v1 (2011-07-01)。物理页 1, 2, 3, 5。
- constrained switching stability。Abstract; Definitions 1.2 and stability result discussion on physical pp. 3–5.。
- 邻近材料。It studies products of matrices along an admissible one-sided subshift and relates constrained joint/generalized spectral radii to uniform exponential stability.。
- 适用范围。There is no occupation measure or stochastic noise model. The relevant compactness and shift-invariance of the admissible signal set cannot be dropped, as its counterexample discussion makes explicit.。

## A Markov jump process associated with the matrix-exponential distribution

- 原件 2f7d8c52497e4774c9cd1b0346adc748ff924f0c1da62f458fa80a16f119d43b。arXiv:2103.02722v1 (3 Mar 2021)。物理页 1, 2。
- matrix-exponential distributions; exponential tilting; finite-state Markov jump processes。pp.1–2, abstract and assumptions A1–A2。
- 邻近材料。For a matrix-exponential density f(x)=αe^{Tx}s, the paper obtains a jump-process interpretation after exponential tilting. Its stated parameter assumptions are real α,T,s and a real strictly negative dominant eigenvalue of T; phase-type laws are a strict subclass.。
- 适用范围。The construction is a representation result for this finite-dimensional matrix family, not a generic saddlepoint or exponential-tilting approximation. Positivity/normalization and the required sufficiently large tilt remain necessary.。

## A Multi-Marginal C-Convex Duality Theorem for Martingale Optimal Transport

- 原件 fc018a7855d39ff14feb63bcc534371e76de09161779fbd366795dc7b112d73d。arXiv:2305.03344v3 (5 Mar 2024)。物理页 1, 2。
- multi-period martingale optimal-transport duality。PDF p. 1, abstract and equations (1.1)–(1.2); p. 2, introduction。
- 邻近材料。The paper extends a c-convex dual formulation from two-marginal to multi-period martingale optimal transport with prescribed one-dimensional marginals.。
- 适用范围。Its c-convex construction is specialized to its MOT formulation; it is not a generic finite-dimensional Farkas certificate or unrestricted transport-duality theorem.。

## A Thomson-type variational principle for diffusion coefficients

- 原件 c1890f8e83ddc177d3c9f20c8be7940e2b81d296715f16a7d87f88f678306482。arXiv:2606.04636v2 (30 Jun 2026)。物理页 1, 2。
- reversible interacting particle systems; diffusion coefficients; variational flows。pp.1–2, abstract and introduction; Theorem 3.7 is identified but not read。
- 邻近材料。The paper derives a supremum-over-flows Thomson principle for the diffusion matrix of reversible conserved-particle systems and illustrates it for a kinetically constrained lattice gas; test flows give lower bounds, complementing Dirichlet-principle upper bounds.。
- 适用范围。The result is for the paper’s reversible conserved-particle framework and its flow constraints. It is neither a Gibbs-entropy duality theorem nor a generic diffusion-coefficient estimator without the generator, invariant law, and flow admissibility checks.。

## A variational representation and large deviations for functionals of G-Brownian motion

- 原件 8a1bec227268a78539410b656a9bb7a5c6daa7f365a6f731ec24a2a045605cd9。arXiv:1204.4525v1 (20 Apr 2012)。物理页 1, 2, 6, 14, 23。
- G-expectation variational representation。Abstract; p. 6, G-Girsanov formula and Theorem 3.1; p. 14, Section 4 setup; p. 23, stochastic-flow assumptions.。
- 邻近材料。The source proves a variational representation for bounded G-Brownian functionals and applies it to an LDP framework and small-noise G-SDE flows under the displayed Lipschitz and convergence hypotheses.。
- 适用范围。Sublinear G-expectation, quadratic-variation uncertainty, and the particular control spaces are essential. It cannot be substituted for a classical probability-space concentration or moderate-deviation routine.。

## A Viscosity Solution Theory of Stochastic Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman Equations in the Wasserstein Space

- 原件 c72ab0d8c833fb1fddc034c43c680cf75c2fc51240a95530df75496bd5537f34。arXiv:2310.14446v1 (2023-10-22)。物理页 1, 2, 12, 29。
- controlled McKean–Vlasov HJB。Abstract and controlled state model on physical pp. 1–2; viscosity construction and comparison/proof sections pp. 12 and 29.。
- 构件候选。The model has idiosyncratic and common Brownian noises, compact control set, and conditional law state. It proposes viscosity solutions for the corresponding HJB in Wasserstein space.。
- 适用范围。The source must not be read as an ordinary state-space HJB theorem: conditional law given common noise, square-integrability, compact actions, and the stated coefficient conditions drive the result.。

## Actuator Dynamics Compensation in Stabilization of Abstract Linear Systems

- 原件 42be6b04b99d8d4854736e1ccfefe6c8a4df5623bceadfc45399be3b3a8030d9。arXiv:2008.11333v1 (26 Aug 2020)。物理页 1。
- stabilization of infinite-dimensional cascade systems。Abstract and §1, physical PDF p. 1.。
- 邻近材料。The source develops full-state-feedback stabilization for cascade abstract systems with possibly infinite-dimensional actuator dynamics, using an upper-block-triangular transform and proving closed-loop exponential stability in its framework.。
- 适用范围。The model is actuator compensation and Sylvester-equation based, rather than an occupation-measure LP or the nonlocal HJB setting of the routed skills.。

## Adaptive sparse grids for time dependent Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equations in stochastic control

- 原件 e3251aadca7b6647f8ba747fcb78092a2254e560ca45dfd1b06a086a98c0bca8。arXiv:1408.4267v1。物理页 1, 14, 16。
- sparse-grid semi-Lagrangian HJB numerics。p. 1 abstract; pp. 14 and 16, interpolation-error sections。
- 邻近材料。The source analyzes sparse-grid interpolation within semi-Lagrangian schemes for time-dependent diffusion HJB equations and states convergence to the viscosity solution under its scheme assumptions.。
- 适用范围。It is a moderate-dimensional numerical method; interpolation monotonicity, grid refinement, and diffusion-control hypotheses prevent use as a general PDE certificate.。

## An Algorithm for Simulating Brownian Increments on a Sphere

- 原件 35c31a7d222239df90bba1a099b2f50b42c1358fb3bb9394ea7e69db5ba0c46b。arXiv:2012.12018v1 (22 Dec 2020)。物理页 1, 2。
- spherical Brownian motion; exact simulation; Wright–Fisher duality。pp.1–2, abstract and equations (2.1)–(2.2)。
- 邻近材料。The source derives an exact-in-law increment simulator for Brownian motion on S^{d−1}(R) from a transition-density representation related to a Wright–Fisher diffusion. The model fixes d≥2, R>0, D>0 and the standard round metric, with reduced time 2Dt/R².。
- 适用范围。The claimed exactness is for spherical Brownian increments under the described representation; floating-point stability limits small time steps, and no Euclidean or general-manifold simulator follows automatically.。

## Approximations of Countably-Infinite Linear Programs over Bounded Measure Spaces

- 原件 92b542665234ed99a83af24bd903a18980895207ca6d1f3e26fc09c10e6dae13。arXiv:1810.03658v3 (1 Dec 2020)。物理页 1。
- finite LP truncations of countably infinite measure-space linear programs。Abstract and §1, physical PDF p. 1.。
- 邻近材料。The paper states finite-dimensional LP approximation schemes with computable errors converging to zero for a bounded class of countably infinite LPs, motivated by stationary, occupation, and exit measures of countable-state Markov chains.。
- 适用范围。Its error claims require the paper's bounded measure-space and moment-bound assumptions; the installed E01 route does not presently encode this countable-state truncation theory.。

## Asymptotics of the Invariant Measure in Mean Field Models with Jumps

- 原件 6fe71a3ceded84177035f7fc6fc724176fd534892b7bb91cbd6eabe5b91d7a80。arXiv:1107.4142v3; Stochastic Systems 2 (2012), DOI 10.1214/12-SSY64。物理页 1, 9。

## Berry--Esseen bound and precise moderate deviations for products of random matrices

- 原件 7eebacf2a7d9f6f85ee0bc0d0e41105163670ca4c1360e15e66e220b5e6a1b6c。arXiv:1907.02438v3 (19 Feb 2025)。物理页 1, 3。
- random-matrix-product precise deviations。Abstract; p. 3, equations (1.2)-(1.5).。
- 邻近材料。Under the paper's random-matrix assumptions, it gives uniform-in-projective-starting-point Cramér expansions for the norm cocycle, plus Berry--Esseen, Edgeworth, and local-limit results obtained through transfer-operator spectral methods.。
- 适用范围。The expansion contains the model's Cramér series and requires its irreducibility/proximality, moment, and spectral-gap hypotheses. It is not a plug-in concentration result for arbitrary matrix products.。

- 原件 cfcf483d74e94ec2ed6849a7273b97a74d7a71b0f1921bae6062221a72274b1f。arXiv:2003.12937v2 (2 Jan 2021)。物理页 1。

## Cramér Type Moderate Deviation for the Maximum of the Periodogram with Application to Simultaneous Tests in Gene Expression Time Series

- 原件 7d9b117a8b59759031afc5326acc9a3233aa6d051040ead74ec722dc4e08eb64。arXiv:0908.1145v1 (10 Aug 2009)。物理页 1, 2, 3。
- periodogram-maximum precise deviations。Abstract; pp. 2-3, equations (1.2)-(1.3) and Theorems 2.1-2.3.。
- 邻近材料。For iid series, the paper gives relative-error approximation of the maximum periodogram tail by its Gumbel form; the reported admissible range changes with tail moments, while the studentized version attains o(n^(1/3)) under a fourth moment.。
- 适用范围。The object is the maximum over Fourier frequencies of an iid series, rather than a partial sum. Moment hypotheses and the simultaneous-testing calibration G=exp(o(n^(1/3))) cannot be transplanted to arbitrary time series.。

## Doob-type optional sampling theorems for demimartingales with applications to associated sequences

- 原件 5a7bb4e396c37014b10257afdb1b6fd251fbd2433a62872d1f5368ed01b8ac13。arXiv:2507.05023v4 (6 Aug 2026; manuscript dated 7 Aug 2026)。物理页 1, 2。

- 构件候选。For the finite-horizon impulse-control QVI min{V_t+H(t,x,V_x), N[V]−V}=0, the authors show that the usual supersolution condition need not imply the obstacle constraint needed for sub/supersolution comparison. - Gaussian fields on spheres; excursion probabilities。abstract p. 1; setup p. 2; Chan–Lai input discussion p. 6。
